```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — Phase-Gated Compound Sparsity\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-16 (数值对齐 registry: DS median_diff 恢复 0.32/1.15/3.97/9.94; Cohen's d 改 registry cohens_d 0.61→1.13/0.37→1.44; C7+C10 status→confirmed; imp/rand 2.7-15×) | 维护者: Director\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nCompound sparsity（attention head pruning + KV cache eviction）的 severity 不是固定常数——它是 eviction strategy 和 reasoning phase 交互的涌现属性。Importance-based eviction 的 compound interaction severity（paired median difference: median(II_low_i − II_high_i)）随 sparsity 单调递增（DeepSeek: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Qwen: 0.048→0.208→1.109→2.619），而 random masking 在 s015/s020 NS，在 s025 跨模型显著但效应量远小于 importance（DS: 0.257 vs 3.986; QW: 0.393 vs 1.074, 2.7-15×差异），存在 sparsity threshold ~s020-s025（D063; s030 待定）。Cohen's d（per-problem paired, B=10000）从 medium 到 large（DeepSeek: 0.61→1.13; Qwen: 0.37→1.44，registry cohens_d s015→s030）。LLaMA 跨架构验证（D060）qualitative direction consistent（within-importance d_z~1.0），但 strategy×phase 交互弱于 Qwen2 架构（imp_vs_rand d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 显著），且 random 在 LLaMA 上也显示显著 phase gradient（d_z 0.35→0.44），与 DeepSeek/Qwen random 低 sparsity NS 模式定性不同。GSM8K 跨 benchmark 验证（D075）：importance masking 的 phase gradient 在 GSM8K 上同样单调递增（QW: 0.039→0.158→1.100→2.092; DS: 0.241→0.740→1.992→4.682），但 strategy specificity（importance/random gradient ratio）从 MATH-500 的 15-36× 衰减至 GSM8K 的 2.3-3.8×。注意：\\\"反转\\\"仅在原始 II_low 值上（random > importance），phase gradient 层面 importance ≥ random。Strategy specificity 的 task-dependence 机制是开放问题（见 §6 Discussion）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\\n\\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\\n\\n## Narrative Arc\\n\\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在低 sparsity (s015/s020) NS，在 s025+ 跨模型显著但效应量远小于 importance (2.7-15×)——存在 sparsity threshold (~s020-s025)，低于阈值 compound effect importance-specific，高于阈值两者均触发但 importance 主导。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。LLaMA 跨架构验证已完成（c3_llama, D060）：qualitative direction consistent（importance phase gradient d_z≈1.0），但 imp_vs_rand 交互弱（d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 显著），且 random 也显示非平凡 phase gradient（d_z 0.35→0.44），strategy-dependence 强度 architecture-dependent。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。\\n\\n**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：\\n- MUST: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance metric limitation / importance is broken\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: claim \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II, and at s025+ random is statistically significant (D063). The distinguishing signal is **magnitude gradient** (importance 2.7-15× larger) and **onset threshold** (random NS below ~s020, importance significant from s015+), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |\\n|----|-------|--------|-------------|\\n| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy- and task-modulated | confirmed | MATH-500: importance gradient 15-36× random; GSM8K: ratio 衰减至 2.3-3.8× (D075)。Phase gradient 层面 importance ≥ random 跨 benchmark 成立，但 magnitude ratio task-dependent。Threshold ~s020-s025 (D063) |\\n| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |\\n| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |\\n| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |\\n| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across models | confirmed | DeepSeek + Qwen cross-model replication: 方向一致, Cohen's d ρ=1.0, gradient permutation p=0.043. Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek, onset threshold s≥0.20 (S3→C). |\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | confirmed | c2_n500_deepseek (success) + c2_qw_n500_r3 (success): 两模型 n=500 全 4 sparsity Bonferroni p<0.005, Spearman ρ=1.0 |\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | partially_confirmed | D060: within-importance phase gradient 强(d_z~1.0) ✓，但 imp_vs_rand 交互弱(d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 Bonferroni p=0.029 显著)。**关键**：random 在 LLaMA 上也显示显著 phase gradient(d_z 0.35→0.44, CI 排除零)，与 DeepSeek/Qwen random 低 sparsity NS 模式定性不同。Qualitative direction consistent, quantitative strategy×phase interaction weaker + architecture-dependent。论文禁用 \\\"confirms\\\"，改用 \\\"suggests\\\"/\\\"provides partial support\\\"。 |\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d_z=0.37 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\n| C10 | Phase gradient cross-benchmark robust; strategy specificity task-modulated | confirmed | GSM8K DS gradient 0.241→4.682 单调递增; QW gradient 0.039→2.092; imp/rand ratio 衰减至 2.3-3.8× (D075)。QW random s025+s030 均 success |\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, benchmark scope\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 四层泛化证据：(1) Cross-model: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（同架构不同训练，ρ=1.0）(2) Cross-architecture: c3_llama (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM)，qualitative direction consistent，但需加限定——LLaMA 上 random 也显示 phase gradient，strategy × phase 交互弱于 Qwen2 架构（D060）(3) **Cross-benchmark (D075)**: GSM8K（两模型）phase gradient 单调递增复现（DS: 0.241→4.682; QW: 0.039→2.092），但 strategy specificity ratio 从 MATH-500 15-36× 衰减至 2.3-3.8×，表明 phase modulation 跨 benchmark 稳健而 magnitude modulation task-dependent。Limitations 诚实声明：(a) 7B scale only；(b) strategy specificity 强度 task-dependent（GSM8K 衰减，可能因推理链更短）；(c) LLaMA 跨架构验证 n=50 限制功效；(d) random phase gradient 行为 architecture-dependent。\\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (cross-model) + C8 (c3_llama cross-architecture) + C10 (cross-benchmark GSM8K, D075)\\n\\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\\n\\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\\n  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。\\n  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。\\n- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)\\n\\n### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)；n=500 实验进一步验证 (C7 running)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Dose-response 主用 Wilcoxon signed-rank p + median_diff（robust to outliers），Cohen's d 作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.61→1.13, Qwen 0.37→1.44, registry cohens_d s015→s030），注释高 sparsity 下 d 受 outlier-driven variance 影响（DS s020→s025: median 3.3× jump 但 d 非单调，因极端 II 值影响分母，D064）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。\\n- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) + n=500 Wilcoxon (C7 running)\\n\\n### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\\n\\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 \\\"phase\\\" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。\\n  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。\\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分\\n\\n## Paper Positioning\\n\\n**类型**: Empirical fi\\n\\n... Discussion）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\\\\n\\\\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\\\\n\\\\n## Narrative Arc\\\\n\\\\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在低 sparsity (s015/s020) NS，在 s025+ 跨模型显著但效应量远小于 importance (2.7-15×)——存在 sparsity threshold (~s020-s025)，低于阈值 compound effect importance-specific，高于阈值两者均触发但 importance 主导。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。LLaMA 跨架构验证已完成（c3_llama, D060）：qualitative direction consistent（importance phase gradient d_z≈1.0），但 imp_vs_rand 交互弱（d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 显著），且 random 也显示非平凡 phase gradient（d_z 0.35→0.44），strategy-dependence 强度 architecture-dependent。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。\\\\n\\\\n**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：\\\\n- MUST: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"emergent phase-strategy \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II, and at s025+ random is statistically significant (D063). The distinguishing signal is **magnitude gradient** (importance 2.7-15× larger) and **onset threshold** (random NS below ~s020, importance significant from s015+), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims\\\\n\\\\n| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |\\\\n|----|-------|--------|-------------|\\\\n| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy- and task-modulated | confirmed | MATH-500: importance gradient 15-36× random; GSM8K: ratio 衰减至 2.3-3.8× (D075)。Phase gradient 层面 importance ≥ random 跨 benchmark 成立，但 magnitude ratio task-dependent。Threshold ~s020-s025 (D063) |\\\\n| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |\\\\n| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |\\\\n| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |\\\\n| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across models | confirmed | DeepSeek + Qwen cross-model replication: 方向一致, Cohen's d ρ=1.0, gradient permutation p=0.043. Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek, onset threshold s≥0.20 (S3→C). |\\\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |\\\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | confirmed | c2_n500_deepseek (success) + c2_qw_n500_r3 (success): 两模型 n=500 全 4 sparsity Bonferroni p<0.005, Spearman ρ=1.0 |\\\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | partially_confirmed | D060: within-importance phase gradient 强(d_z~1.0) ✓，但 imp_vs_rand 交互弱(d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 Bonferroni p=0.029 显著)。**关键**：random 在 LLaMA 上也显示显著 phase gradient(d_z 0.35→0.44, CI 排除零)，与 DeepSeek/Qwen random 低 sparsity NS 模式定性不同。Qualitative direction consistent, quantitative strategy×phase interaction weaker + architecture-dependent。论文禁用 \\\\\\\"confirms\\\\\\\"，改用 \\\\\\\"suggests\\\\\\\"/\\\\\\\"provides partial support\\\\\\\"。 |\\\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d_z=0.37 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\\\n| C10 | Phase gradient cross-benchmark robust; strategy specificity task-modulated | confirmed | GSM8K DS gradient 0.241→4.682 单调递增; QW gradient 0.039→2.092; imp/rand ratio 衰减至 2.3-3.8× (D075)。QW random s025+s030 均 success |\\\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\\\n\\\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\\\n\\\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, benchmark scope\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: 四层泛化证据：(1) Cross-model: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（同架构不同训练，ρ=1.0）(2) Cross-architecture: c3_llama (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM)，qualitative direction consistent，但需加限定——LLaMA 上 random 也显示 phase gradient，strategy × phase 交互弱于 Qwen2 架构（D060）(3) **Cross-benchmark (D075)**: GSM8K（两模型）phase gradient 单调递增复现（DS: 0.241→4.682; QW: 0.039→2.092），但 strategy specificity ratio 从 MATH-500 15-36× 衰减至 2.3-3.8×，表明 phase modulation 跨 benchmark 稳健而 magnitude modulation task-dependent。Limitations 诚实声明：(a) 7B scale only；(b) strategy specificity 强度 task-dependent（GSM8K 衰减，可能因推理链更短）；(c) LLaMA 跨架构验证 n=50 限制功效；(d) random phase gradient 行为 architecture-dependent。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (cross-model) + C8 (c3_llama cross-architecture) + C10 (cross-benchmark GSM8K, D075)\\\\n\\\\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\\\\n\\\\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\\\\n  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。\\\\n  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)\\\\n\\\\n### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)；n=500 实验进一步验证 (C7 running)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Dose-response 主用 Wilcoxon signed-rank p + median_diff（robust to outliers），Cohen's d 作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.61→1.13, Qwen 0.37→1.44, registry cohens_d s015→s030），注释高 sparsity 下 d 受 outlier-driven variance 影响（DS s020→s025: median 3.3× jump 但 d 非单调，因极端 II 值影响分母，D064）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) + n=500 Wilcoxon (C7 running)\\\\n\\\\n### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\\\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\\\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\\\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\\\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\\\\n  - DS: 0.241→0.740→1.992→4.682），但 strategy specificity（importance/random gradient ratio）从 MATH-500 的 15-36× 衰减至 GSM8K 的 2.3-3.8×。注意：\\\"反转\\\"仅在原始 II_low 值上（random > importance），phase gradient 层面 importance ≥ random。Strategy specificity 的 task-dependence 机制是开放问题（见 §6 Discussion）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\\n\\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\\n\\n## Narrative Arc\\n\\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在低 sparsity (s015/s020) NS，在 s025+ 跨模型显著但效应量远小于 importance (2.7-15×)——存在 sparsity threshold (~s020-s025)，低于阈值 compound effect importance-specific，高于阈值两者均触发但 importance 主导。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。LLaMA 跨架构验证已完成（c3_llama, D060）：qualitative direction consistent（importance phase gradient d_z≈1.0），但 imp_vs_rand 交互弱（d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 显著），且 random 也显示非平凡 phase gradient（d_z 0.35→0.44），strategy-dependence 强度 architecture-dependent。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。\\n\\n**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：\\n- MUST: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance metric limitation / importance is broken\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: claim \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II, and at s025+ random is statistically significant (D063). The distinguishing signal is **magnitude gradient** (importance 2.7-15× larger) and **onset threshold** (random NS below ~s020, importance significant from s015+), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |\\n|----|-------|--------|-------------|\\n| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy- and task-modulated | confirmed | MATH-500: importance gradient 15-36× random; GSM8K: ratio 衰减至 2.3-3.8× (D075)。Phase gradient 层面 importance ≥ random 跨 benchmark 成立，但 magnitude ratio task-dependent。Threshold ~s020-s025 (D063) |\\n| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |\\n| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |\\n| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |\\n| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across models | confirmed | DeepSeek + Qwen cross-model replication: 方向一致, Cohen's d ρ=1.0, gradient permutation p=0.043. Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek, onset threshold s≥0.20 (S3→C). |\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | confirmed | c2_n500_deepseek (success) + c2_qw_n500_r3 (success): 两模型 n=500 全 4 sparsity Bonferroni p<0.005, Spearman ρ=1.0 |\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | partially_confirmed | D060: within-importance phase gradient 强(d_z~1.0) ✓，但 imp_vs_rand 交互弱(d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 Bonferroni p=0.029 显著)。**关键**：random 在 LLaMA 上也显示显著 phase gradient(d_z 0.35→0.44, CI 排除零)，与 DeepSeek/Qwen random 低 sparsity NS 模式定性不同。Qualitative direction consistent, quantitative strategy×phase interaction weaker + architecture-dependent。论文禁用 \\\"confirms\\\"，改用 \\\"suggests\\\"/\\\"provides partial support\\\"。 |\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d_z=0.37 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\n| C10 | Phase gradient cross-benchmark robust; strategy specificity task-modulated | confirmed | GSM8K DS gradient 0.241→4.682 单调递增; QW gradient 0.039→2.092; imp/rand ratio 衰减至 2.3-3.8× (D075)。QW random s025+s030 均 success |\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, benchmark scope\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 四层泛化证据：(1) Cross-model: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（同架构不同训练，ρ=1.0）(2) Cross-architecture: c3_llama (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM)，qualitative direction consistent，但需加限定——LLaMA 上 random 也显示 phase gradient，strategy × phase 交互弱于 Qwen2 架构（D060）(3) **Cross-benchmark (D075)**: GSM8K（两模型）phase gradient 单调递增复现（DS: 0.241→4.682; QW: 0.039→2.092），但 strategy specificity ratio 从 MATH-500 15-36× 衰减至 2.3-3.8×，表明 phase modulation 跨 benchmark 稳健而 magnitude modulation task-dependent。Limitations 诚实声明：(a) 7B scale only；(b) strategy specificity 强度 task-dependent（GSM8K 衰减，可能因推理链更短）；(c) LLaMA 跨架构验证 n=50 限制功效；(d) random phase gradient 行为 architecture-dependent。\\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (cross-model) + C8 (c3_llama cross-architecture) + C10 (cross-benchmark GSM8K, D075)\\n\\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\\n\\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\\n  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。\\n  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。\\n- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)\\n\\n### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)；n=500 实验进一步验证 (C7 running)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Dose-response 主用 Wilcoxon signed-rank p + median_diff（robust to outliers），Cohen's d 作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.61→1.13, Qwen 0.37→1.44, registry cohens_d s015→s030），注释高 sparsity 下 d 受 outlier-driven variance 影响（DS s020→s025: median 3.3× jump 但 d 非单调，因极端 II 值影响分母，D064）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。\\n- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) + n=500 Wilcoxon (C7 running)\\n\\n### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\\n\\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 \\\"phase\\\" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。\\n  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。\\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分\\n\\n## Paper Positioning\\n\\n**类型**: Empirical fi\\n\\n... <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01M5J5zaZmKjXAuQXSQraJmY`</summary>
```

```text
1	\section{Compound Sparsity Formulation}
2	\label{app:formulation-details}
3	
4	\paragraph{Attention head pruning.}
5	Heads are ranked by Frobenius norm importance $w_j^{(\ell)} = \|\mathbf{A}_j^{(\ell)}\|_F$ \cite{voita-acl2019, michel-neurips2019}; a binary mask $m_j^{(\ell)} \in \{0,1\}$ retains the top-$(1{-}s)\cdot H$ heads:
6	\begin{equation}
7	    \tilde{\mathbf{o}}_j^{(\ell)} = m_j^{(\ell)} \cdot \mathbf{o}_j^{(\ell)}.
8	    \label{eq:head-mask}
9	\end{equation}
10	
11	\paragraph{KV cache eviction.}
12	A position mask $\mathbf{m}_k \in \{0,1\}^T$ retains the top-$(1{-}s)\cdot T$ positions (BOS always preserved):
13	\begin{equation}
14	    \tilde{a}_{ij} = a_{ij} \cdot m_{k,j}, \quad \forall\, i, j.
15	    \label{eq:kv-mask}
16	\end{equation}
17	Under importance-based eviction, each position $j$ is scored by its cumulative attention mass with position normalization:
18	\begin{equation}
19	    \mathrm{imp}(j) = \frac{\sum_{\ell, h, i} a_{ij}^{(\ell,h)}}{T - j},
20	    \label{eq:kv-importance}
21	\end{equation}
22	where the denominator compensates for causal bias---earlier positions are attended to by more queries \cite{thinkv-iclr2026, defensivekv-iclr2026}. Under random eviction, positions (excluding BOS) are evicted uniformly at random.
23	
24	\paragraph{Compound sparsity.}
25	Applying both mechanisms simultaneously yields compound sparsity. The two axes interact: head pruning redistributes attention mass, altering the importance ranking for KV eviction. Prior work \cite{spark-aaai2026} reports super-additive joint effects but treats interaction magnitude as a fixed property of $s$.
26	
27	\paragraph{Attention sparsity metric.}
28	For each query position $i$ and head $h$, we compute the fraction of key positions receiving negligible attention:
29	\begin{equation}
30	    \sigma_i^{(h)} = \frac{1}{T} \sum_{j=1}^{T} \mathbf{1}\!\left[a_{ij}^{(h)} < 0.01 \cdot \max_k a_{ik}^{(h)}\right].
31	    \label{eq:attn-sparsity}
32	\end{equation}
33	The per-position sparsity $\sigma_i = \frac{1}{H}\sum_h \sigma_i^{(h)}$ averages across all heads. Tokens are partitioned into high-, mid-, and low-sparsity phases by the tercile boundaries of $\{\sigma_i\}$; segments shorter than 10 tokens are merged into adjacent phases.
34	
35	\section{Multiplicative Null Verification}
36	\label{app:multiplicative-null}
37	
38	We verified that replacing the additive null $r_b = r_a + r_k - 1$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:ii}) with the multiplicative null $r_b = r_a \cdot r_k$ does not change qualitative conclusions. Under the multiplicative formulation, the interaction index becomes $\mathrm{II}_{\times} = r_b - r_a \cdot r_k$. Notably, $\mathrm{II}_{\times} < 0$ across all phases and sparsity levels: the compound interaction is \emph{sub-multiplicative} everywhere---actual compound degradation is less severe than the multiplicative prediction. $G_{\times}$ therefore quantifies the ratio of sub-multiplicative deviations between phases, not super-multiplicative amplification: a value of $G_{\times}{=}30$ means the low-sparsity phase deviates from the multiplicative null by $30{\times}$ more than the high-sparsity phase. Under importance-based eviction, $G_{\times}{=}21.1$--$36.2{\times}$ (DeepSeek) and $43.9$--$64.0{\times}$ (Qwen) across $s \in \{0.15, 0.20, 0.25, 0.30\}$, yielding a combined range of $21$--$64{\times}$. Although compound effects do not exceed the multiplicative prediction in any phase, the low-sparsity phase's deviation from the multiplicative null is $21$--$64{\times}$ larger than the high-sparsity phase's, validating that the phase-gated severity gradient is preserved under the multiplicative null. These $n{=}500$ exact values are more conservative than the preliminary $n{=}50$ calibrated estimates ($\approx$51--183${\times}$), reflecting reduced variance at full sample size. Values are computed using aggregate per-phase PPL ratios; per-problem exact computation is left for future work. Random eviction remains flat ($G_{\times} \approx 1$). The crossover at $s{=}0.25$ is preserved. We adopt the additive formulation in the main text as the more conservative baseline: it assumes zero interaction (compound loss equals the sum of individual losses), so rejection ($\mathrm{II} > 0$) establishes super-additivity regardless of null specification. The qualitative conclusions---phase-modulated severity, strategy asymmetry, and crossover---are preserved under the multiplicative null.
39	
40	% Bootstrap and permutation details moved to main text \S\ref{sec:stat-validation}
41
```

```text
2	\section{Phase-Strategy Crossover Details}
3	\label{app:crossover-details}
4	
5	On DeepSeek ($n{=}496$), a strategy ranking reversal emerges at $s{=}0.25$ (Figure~\ref{fig:crossover}): importance-based eviction yields \emph{lower} II than random in the high-sparsity phase ($0.021$ vs.\ $1.113$) but \emph{higher} II in the low-sparsity phase ($3.971$ vs.\ $1.514$---a $2.6{\times}$ reversal; both $p < 10^{-4}$). This crossover is confirmed at all four sparsity levels, with reversal magnitude of $4.0{\times}$ at $s{=}0.15$, $3.4{\times}$ at $s{=}0.20$, $2.6{\times}$ at $s{=}0.25$, and $2.2{\times}$ at $s{=}0.30$ (decreasing as random eviction severity rises). The reversal arises because head pruning redistributes attention mass, altering importance rankings in a way that couples the two sparsity mechanisms in low-sparsity phases (\S\ref{sec:discrimination-collapse-main}). Qwen does not exhibit a crossover: importance-based eviction produces lower II than random in both phases across all sparsity levels (e.g., at $s{=}0.25$: high $0.004$ vs.\ $1.108$; low $1.053$ vs.\ $1.769$).

101	\section{Permutation Test Details}
102	\label{app:permutation-details}
103	
104	For each problem $i$ and sparsity level $s$, we compute the compound severity difference $\Delta_i^{(\text{strat})} = \mathrm{II}_{\textit{low},i} - \mathrm{II}_{\textit{high},i}$ under each eviction strategy. The test statistic is the median of the paired difference $\widetilde{\Delta} = \text{median}_i\!\bigl(\Delta_i^{(\text{imp})} - \Delta_i^{(\text{rand})}\bigr)$, measuring whether importance-based eviction produces systematically larger phase-dependent severity than random eviction. We permute strategy labels $10{,}000$ times to construct the null distribution and apply Bonferroni correction for 4 sparsity-level comparisons.
105	
106	Per-problem distributions of the paired severity difference $\Delta_i = \mathrm{II}_{\textit{low},i} - \mathrm{II}_{\textit{high},i}$ under importance-based eviction are right-skewed at all sparsity levels, with mean/median ratios ranging from $1.2{\times}$ ($s{=}0.15$) to $1.6{\times}$ ($s{=}0.30$) for DeepSeek and $1.1{\times}$ to $1.4{\times}$ for Qwen.
107	
108	The permutation null distributions are approximately symmetric around zero at all sparsity levels. At $s{=}0.15$, the observed statistic falls at the $99.92$th percentile of the null ($p_{\text{Bonf}} = 0.0032$); at $s \geq 0.20$, no permutation exceeds the observed statistic.
109	
110	Table~\ref{tab:permutation} reports the results. All four sparsity levels reject $H_0$ after Bonferroni correction ($p_{\text{Bonf}} < 0.005$), with observed median differences increasing monotonically from $0.37$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $9.85$ at $s{=}0.30$. The monotonic growth of both the point estimate and the CI lower bound ($0.19 \to 0.69 \to 1.66 \to 6.70$) confirms that compound severity amplification strengthens with sparsity---not merely in central tendency but also in worst-case lower bounds. Figure~\ref{fig:permtest-forest} presents these results as a forest plot.

137	\section{Cross-Architecture Analysis}
138	\label{app:cross-arch}
139	
140	To test whether the phase-modulated severity pattern extends beyond the Qwen2 architecture, we replicate the experiment on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B (LlamaForCausalLM, 8B parameters; $n{=}49$ paired problems). Table~\ref{tab:llama-cross-arch} reports the strategy-dependent severity comparison across sparsity levels.
141	
142	The evidence for a strategy contrast on LLaMA is mixed: at $s{=}0.15$, the bootstrap CI excludes zero ($[0.002, 0.043]$) but the Bonferroni-corrected Wilcoxon test is non-significant ($p_{\text{Bonf}}{=}0.219$); conversely, at $s{=}0.30$ the CI spans zero ($[-0.069, 0.544]$) while the Wilcoxon test reaches significance ($p_{\text{Bonf}}{=}0.029$). At intermediate levels ($s \in \{0.20, 0.25\}$), neither CI nor Wilcoxon reaches significance. The median paired difference increases monotonically ($\widetilde{\Delta}$: $0.020 \to 0.356$), consistent with a genuine but weak trend---providing preliminary support for the cross-architecture hypothesis while underscoring the substantially weaker interaction on LlamaForCausalLM.\footnote{The CI/Wilcoxon discrepancy reflects differing power profiles under small $n$ with heavy-tailed distributions: the percentile bootstrap CI is based on the resampled median distribution and is less sensitive to tail observations, while the Wilcoxon signed-rank test uses signed ranks and is more sensitive to tail outliers. This inconsistency itself supports the conservative ``mixed/weak evidence'' interpretation.}
143	
144	The absolute magnitude is architecture-dependent: LLaMA severity differences are ${\sim}40{\times}$ smaller than DeepSeek and ${\sim}7{\times}$ smaller than Qwen. Notably, random eviction on LLaMA also exhibits a non-trivial phase gradient ($d_z$: $0.35 \to 0.44$), substantially reducing the strategy contrast that is the hallmark of the phase-strategy interaction on Qwen2 models. This suggests that the strength of the strategy$\times$phase interaction---not merely the severity gradient itself---is architecture-dependent. The directional pattern---importance producing larger phase gradients than random---is qualitatively consistent across all three models, but the LLaMA evidence should be interpreted as preliminary given the weak interaction magnitude and partial statistical significance.

163	\paragraph{Post-hoc power analysis.}
164	We computed post-hoc power via bootstrap simulation ($B{=}10{,}000$) to assess what claims the $n{=}49$ sample can and cannot support. For the within-importance phase gradient (testing $\Delta_{\text{imp}} > 0$), power exceeds $0.99$ at all four sparsity levels, confirming that the sample is sufficient to detect the importance-based severity gradient. For strategy comparisons (testing $\Delta_{\text{imp}} - \Delta_{\text{rand}} > 0$), power ranges from $0.26$ ($s{=}0.15$) to $0.63$ ($s{=}0.30$), indicating that the sample is underpowered for detecting strategy-level differences at their observed effect sizes. Extrapolating from the observed effect sizes and variance, $n \geq 200$ paired problems would be needed to achieve $0.80$ power for the strategy comparison at $s{=}0.30$. This motivates the conservative interpretation in \S\ref{sec:cross-arch}: the within-importance gradient is confirmed, but the strategy$\times$phase interaction requires larger-scale replication.
165	
166	
167	\section{Analysis: Importance Discrimination Collapse}
168	\label{app:analysis}
169	
170	\subsection{Importance Discrimination Collapse}
171	\label{sec:discrimination-collapse}
172	
173	Head pruning reduces the number of heads contributing to cumulative importance scores, collapsing the score distribution. Table~\ref{tab:discrimination-metrics-full} quantifies this effect: the Gini coefficient decreases by $0.030$--$0.081$ (DeepSeek) and $0.010$--$0.020$ (Qwen) after pruning, with consistent negative deltas across all 16 conditions (both models $\times$ 4 sparsity levels $\times$ 2 strategies). The CV delta is likewise negative in all 8 importance-based conditions. The same pattern holds under random eviction, confirming that importance discrimination collapse is a consequence of head pruning \emph{per se}, not of the eviction strategy.
174	
175	\begin{table}[t]
176	\centering
177	\caption{Importance discrimination collapse under importance-based eviction. $\Delta\mathrm{Gini}$ and $\Delta\mathrm{CV}$ are post-pruning minus baseline; negative values indicate reduced discriminative power.}
178	\label{tab:discrimination-metrics-full}
179	\small
180	\begin{tabular}{llrr}
181	\toprule
182	Model & $s$ & $\Delta\mathrm{Gini}$ & $\Delta\mathrm{CV}$ \\
183	\midrule
184	DeepSeek & $0.15$ & $-0.030$ & $-0.56$ \\
185	DeepSeek & $0.20$ & $-0.040$ & $-0.77$ \\
186	DeepSeek & $0.25$ & $-0.065$ & $-1.29$ \\
187	DeepSeek & $0.30$ & $-0.081$ & $-1.66$ \\
188	\midrule
189	Qwen & $0.15$ & $-0.010$ & $-0.05$ \\
190	Qwen & $0.20$ & $-0.012$ & $-0.21$ \\
191	Qwen & $0.25$ & $-0.010$ & $-1.49$ \\
192	Qwen & $0.30$ & $-0.020$ & $-1.14$ \\
193	\bottomrule
194	\end{tabular}
195	\end{table}
196	
197	When importance scores lose discriminability, thresholding degenerates into noise-sensitive selection---functionally approaching biased random eviction. This is phase-dependent: in low-sparsity phases, attention is already broadly distributed, so pruning-induced collapse has maximum impact; in high-sparsity phases, concentration is preserved. Random eviction is immune because its decisions are independent of score quality, explaining $G \approx 1$.
198	
199	\subsection{Entropy Redistribution}
200	\label{sec:entropy-redistribution}
201	
202	Beyond importance discrimination collapse, we examine whether head pruning also redistributes the attention entropy of surviving heads. We measure the mean attention entropy per head:
203	\begin{equation}
204	    \mathcal{H}_i^{(h)} = -\sum_{j=1}^{T} a_{ij}^{(h)} \log a_{ij}^{(h)}
205	    \label{eq:attn-entropy}
206	\end{equation}
207	and compute $\Delta\mathcal{H}$ as the change from baseline (no pruning) to the pruned condition.
208	
209	For DeepSeek under importance-based eviction, the entropy delta is consistently negative across all four sparsity levels ($\Delta\mathcal{H} \in [-0.047, -0.040]$; Table~\ref{tab:entropy-deltas}), indicating that importance-based pruning systematically selects heads whose removal concentrates attention in the surviving heads, reinforcing importance discrimination collapse.
210	
211	For Qwen, the entropy pattern only partially replicates. The delta is negative at $s{=}0.15$ ($\Delta\mathcal{H} = -0.073$) and $s{=}0.20$ ($-0.076$), consistent with DeepSeek. However, at $s{=}0.25$, the delta reverses to $+0.045$, with 22 of 30 problems showing positive entropy change. At $s{=}0.30$, the delta is near zero ($-0.007$). The $s{=}0.25$ reversal is systematic rather than a statistical artifact: it is consistent across all three reasoning phases (low: $+0.042$, mid: $+0.034$, high: $+0.017$). We interpret this as a genuine model-specific difference, possibly arising from distinct head specialization patterns: Qwen's instruction tuning (vs.\ DeepSeek's distillation-based training) may produce head configurations where removing 25\% of heads triggers compensatory broadening rather than concentration.
212	
213	We characterize importance discrimination collapse as the robust, model-independent driver of the phase-strategy interaction, with entropy redistribution as a secondary amplifier that is consistent in DeepSeek but model-dependent in Qwen.
214	
215	\begin{table}[t]
216	\centering
217	\caption{Attention entropy deltas ($\Delta\mathcal{H}$) under importance-based eviction. DeepSeek shows consistent entropy compression; Qwen partially replicates with a reversal at $s{=}0.25$.}
218	\label{tab:entropy-deltas}
219	\small
220	\begin{tabular}{llr}
221	\toprule
222	Model & $s$ & $\Delta\mathcal{H}$ \\
223	\midrule
224	DeepSeek & $0.15$ & $-0.040$ \\
225	DeepSeek & $0.20$ & $-0.047$ \\
226	DeepSeek & $0.25$ & $-0.045$ \\
227	DeepSeek & $0.30$ & $-0.044$ \\
228	\midrule
229	Qwen & $0.15$ & $-0.073$ \\
230	Qwen & $0.20$ & $-0.076$ \\
231	Qwen & $0.25$ & $+0.045$ \\
232	Qwen & $0.30$ & $-0.007$ \\
233	\bottomrule
234	\end{tabular}
235	\end{table}
```

```json
      "content": "\n\\section{Phase-Strategy Crossover Details}\n\\label{app:crossover-details}\n\nOn DeepSeek ($n{=}496$), a strategy ranking reversal emerges at $s{=}0.25$ (Figure~\\ref{fig:crossover}): importance-based eviction yields \\emph{lower} II than random in the high-sparsity phase ($0.021$ vs.\\ $1.113$) but \\emph{higher} II in the low-sparsity phase ($3.971$ vs.\\ $1.514$---a $2.6{\\times}$ reversal; both $p < 10^{-4}$). This crossover is confirmed at all four sparsity levels, with reversal magnitude of $4.0{\\times}$ at $s{=}0.15$, $3.4{\\times}$ at $s{=}0.20$, $2.6{\\times}$ at $s{=}0.25$, and $2.2{\\times}$ at $s{=}0.30$ (decreasing as random eviction severity rises). The reversal arises because head pruning redistributes attention mass, altering importance rankings in a way that couples the two sparsity mechanisms in low-sparsity phases (\\S\\ref{sec:discrimination-collapse-main}). Qwen does not exhibit a crossover: importance-based eviction produces lower II than random in both phases across all sparsity levels (e.g., at $s{=}0.25$: high $0.004$ vs.\\ $1.108$; low $1.053$ vs.\\ $1.769$).\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{fig3_crossover.pdf}\n\\caption{Strategy-dependent crossover at $s{=}0.25$ on DeepSeek ($n{=}496$). Grouped bar chart of median II by eviction strategy and reasoning phase. Importance-based eviction outperforms random in the high-sparsity phase but produces $2.6{\\times}$ higher II in the low-sparsity phase, reversing the strategy ranking.}\n\\label{fig:crossover}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\n\\section{Profiling Subset Per-Phase Breakdown}\n\\label{app:profiling-subset}\n\n\\begin{table*}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Per-phase interaction index (II) from the $n{=}50$ profiling subset (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B). The main analysis ($n{=}496$) appears in Table~\\ref{tab:main-results}. $G$ values in this table use the ratio-of-medians definition ($\\widetilde{\\mathrm{II}}_{\\textit{low}} / \\widetilde{\\mathrm{II}}_{\\textit{high}}$), which differs from the primary per-problem definition in Eq.~\\ref{eq:gradient}. At low sparsity ($s{=}0.15$), the two definitions diverge substantially ($7.5{\\times}$ vs.\\ $69{\\times}$) because per-problem ratios are sensitive to near-zero $\\mathrm{II}_{\\textit{high}}$ values.}\n\\label{tab:profiling-subset}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}llccccr@{}}\n\\toprule\n$s$ & Strategy & $\\widetilde{\\mathrm{II}}_{\\textit{high}}$ & $\\widetilde{\\mathrm{II}}_{\\textit{mid}}$ & $\\widetilde{\\mathrm{II}}_{\\textit{low}}$ & $\\Delta$ & $G$ \\\\\n\\midrule\n0.15 & Importance & 0.006 & 0.081 & 0.398 & 0.39 & $\\mathbf{69}$ \\\\\n0.15 & Random     & 0.093 & 0.097 & 0.103 & 0.01 & 1.1 \\\\\n\\addlinespace\n0.20 & Importance & 0.015 & 0.203 & 1.496 & 1.48 & $\\mathbf{103}$ \\\\\n0.20 & Random     & 0.292 & 0.350 & 0.276 & $-$0.02 & 0.9 \\\\\n\\addlinespace\n0.25 & Importance & 0.032 & 1.118 & 7.196 & 7.16 & $\\mathbf{222}$ \\\\\n0.25 & Random     & 0.818 & 0.932 & 1.363 & 0.54 & 1.7 \\\\\n\\addlinespace\n0.30 & Importance & 0.053 & 2.283 & 14.639 & 14.59 & $\\mathbf{274}$ \\\\\n0.30 & Random     & 2.503 & 2.592 & 4.041 & 1.54 & 1.6 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table*}\n\n\n\\section{Cross-Training-Paradigm Replication Details}\n\\label{app:cross-model-details}\n\nBoth models share the Qwen2ForCausalLM backbone (28 layers, 28 heads, 4-group GQA) but differ in training: DeepSeek uses reasoning distillation from DeepSeek-R1, while Qwen2.5 uses standard instruction tuning without explicit reasoning delimiters. This within-architecture-family comparison enables assessment of training methodology effects on the compound sparsity interaction, though causal attribution is limited by the observational design.\n\nDespite lacking explicit \\texttt{&lt;think>} delimiters, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct exhibits sufficient variation in attention sparsity to permit phase segmentation via the same quantile-based method (\\S\\ref{par:phase-segmentation}). Twenty-five of the 500 problems are excluded due to degenerate phase assignments, yielding $n{=}475$ valid problems for Qwen (vs.\\ $n{=}496$ for DeepSeek).\n\n\\paragraph{Per-phase effective sample size.}\n\\label{app:qwen-null-rate}\nTable~\\ref{tab:qwen-effective-n} reports the per-phase effective sample size for Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, defined as the number of problems with non-null phase segments (segment length $\\geq$ \\texttt{min\\_phase\\_length}). The mid-phase null rate (45.2\\%) reflects problems where mid-sparsity tokens do not form contiguous runs above the merging threshold; this does not affect the low--high contrast ($\\Delta$) used in the primary analysis.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Per-phase effective sample size for Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($n_{\\text{eff}} = 475$). Phase segmentation is performed once on dense-model attention patterns and is independent of sparsity level.}\n\\label{tab:qwen-effective-n}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}lcccc@{}}\n\\toprule\nPhase & $s{=}0.15$ & $s{=}0.20$ & $s{=}0.25$ & $s{=}0.30$ \\\\\n\\midrule\nHigh & 475 & 475 & 475 & 475 \\\\\nMid  & 274 & 274 & 274 & 274 \\\\\nLow  & 475 & 475 & 475 & 475 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\nTable~\\ref{tab:cross-model} reports the cross-training-paradigm comparison. Qwen reproduces the monotonic severity gradient at ${\\sim}1/5$ of DeepSeek's magnitude. Figure~\\ref{fig:cross-model} visualizes this comparison.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Cross-training-paradigm comparison of median compound severity difference ($\\Delta$) under importance-based eviction.}\n\\label{tab:cross-model}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}lcccc@{}}\n\\toprule\nModel & $s{=}0.15$ & $s{=}0.20$ & $s{=}0.25$ & $s{=}0.30$ \\\\\n\\midrule\nDeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B ($n{=}496$) & 0.39 & 1.21 & 3.99 & 8.48 \\\\\nQwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($n{=}475$)         & 0.07 & 0.31 & 1.07 & 3.16 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{fig5_crossmodel.pdf}\n\\caption{Cross-training-paradigm comparison of the median severity difference under importance-based eviction ($n{=}496$/$475$). Both DeepSeek (solid) and Qwen (dashed) exhibit monotonically increasing $\\Delta$ with sparsity; Qwen's magnitude is approximately one-quarter to one-sixth of DeepSeek's.}\n\\label{fig:cross-model}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\\paragraph{Per-problem concordance.}\nThe per-problem concordance across models is not significant (mean $\\rho = -0.107$, $p = 0.926$), indicating that while the aggregate gradient direction replicates, the specific problems driving severity differ between models---consistent with model-specific attention patterns rather than a universal per-problem vulnerability ranking.\n\n\\paragraph{Random eviction control.}\nAs a control, random eviction on Qwen mirrors DeepSeek: non-significant at $s \\leq 0.20$ ($\\Delta$: $-0.01$, $0.02$; CIs span zero) but significant at $s \\geq 0.25$ ($\\Delta{=}0.39$, $p_{\\text{Bonf}} < 10^{-10}$; $\\Delta{=}0.91$, $p_{\\text{Bonf}} < 10^{-13}$). Where significant, the random phase gradient is $2.7$--$3.5{\\times}$ weaker than importance-based ($0.39$ vs.\\ $1.07$ at $s{=}0.25$; $0.91$ vs.\\ $3.16$ at $s{=}0.30$), confirming strategy-specific amplification.\n\n\n\\section{Permutation Test Details}\n\\label{app:permutation-details}\n\nFor each problem $i$ and sparsity level $s$, we compute the compound severity difference $\\Delta_i^{(\\text{strat})} = \\mathrm{II}_{\\textit{low},i} - \\mathrm{II}_{\\textit{high},i}$ under each eviction strategy. The test statistic is the median of the paired difference $\\widetilde{\\Delta} = \\text{median}_i\\!\\bigl(\\Delta_i^{(\\text{imp})} - \\Delta_i^{(\\text{rand})}\\bigr)$, measuring whether importance-based eviction produces systematically larger phase-dependent severity than random eviction. We permute strategy labels $10{,}000$ times to construct the null distribution and apply Bonferroni correction for 4 sparsity-level comparisons.\n\nPer-problem distributions of the paired severity difference $\\Delta_i = \\mathrm{II}_{\\textit{low},i} - \\mathrm{II}_{\\textit{high},i}$ under importance-based eviction are right-skewed at all sparsity levels, with mean/median ratios ranging from $1.2{\\times}$ ($s{=}0.15$) to $1.6{\\times}$ ($s{=}0.30$) for DeepSeek and $1.1{\\times}$ to $1.4{\\times}$ for Qwen.\n\nThe permutation null distributions are approximately symmetric around zero at all sparsity levels. At $s{=}0.15$, the observed statistic falls at the $99.92$th percentile of the null ($p_{\\text{Bonf}} = 0.0032$); at $s \\geq 0.20$, no permutation exceeds the observed statistic.\n\nTable~\\ref{tab:permutation} reports the results. All four sparsity levels reject $H_0$ after Bonferroni correction ($p_{\\text{Bonf}} < 0.005$), with observed median differences increasing monotonically from $0.37$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $9.85$ at $s{=}0.30$. The monotonic growth of both the point estimate and the CI lower bound ($0.19 \\to 0.69 \\to 1.66 \\to 6.70$) confirms that compound severity amplification strengthens with sparsity---not merely in central tendency but also in worst-case lower bounds. Figure~\\ref{fig:permtest-forest} presents these results as a forest plot.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Paired permutation test for compound severity: importance vs.\\ random eviction on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B ($n{=}49$ profiling subset, $10{,}000$ permutations, Bonferroni-corrected). The monotonic $\\Delta$ pattern is confirmed at $n{=}496$ (Table~\\ref{tab:aggregate-ii}).}\n\\label{tab:permutation}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}rrcl@{}}\n\\toprule\n$s$ & Observed $\\widetilde{\\Delta}$ & 95\\% CI & $p_{\\text{Bonf}}$ \\\\\n\\midrule\n0.15 & 0.37 & $[0.19,\\; 0.62]$ & 0.0032 \\\\\n0.20 & 0.97 & $[0.69,\\; 1.49]$ & ${<}\\,0.001$ \\\\\n0.25 & 3.34 & $[1.66,\\; 6.64]$ & ${<}\\,0.001$ \\\\\n0.30 & 9.85 & $[6.70,\\; 16.29]$ & ${<}\\,0.001$ \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{fig4_bootstrap.pdf}\n\\caption{Forest plot of permutation test results. Horizontal bars show 95\\% permutation CIs; diamonds mark observed median differences $\\widetilde{\\Delta}$. All CIs lie entirely above zero, confirming significant strategy-dependent severity at every sparsity level.}\n\\label{fig:permtest-forest}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\n\\section{Cross-Architecture Analysis}\n\\label{app:cross-arch}\n\nTo test whether the phase-modulated severity pattern extends beyond the Qwen2 architecture, we replicate the experiment on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B (LlamaForCausalLM, 8B parameters; $n{=}49$ paired problems). Table~\\ref{tab:llama-cross-arch} reports the strategy-dependent severity comparison across sparsity levels.\n\nThe evidence for a strategy contrast on LLaMA is mixed: at $s{=}0.15$, the bootstrap CI excludes zero ($[0.002, 0.043]$) but the Bonferroni-corrected Wilcoxon test is non-significant ($p_{\\text{Bonf}}{=}0.219$); conversely, at $s{=}0.30$ the CI spans zero ($[-0.069, 0.544]$) while the Wilcoxon test reaches significance ($p_{\\text{Bonf}}{=}0.029$). At intermediate levels ($s \\in \\{0.20, 0.25\\}$), neither CI nor Wilcoxon reaches significance. The median paired difference increases monotonically ($\\widetilde{\\Delta}$: $0.020 \\to 0.356$), consistent with a genuine but weak trend---providing preliminary support for the cross-architecture hypothesis while underscoring the substantially weaker interaction on LlamaForCausalLM.\\footnote{The CI/Wilcoxon discrepancy reflects differing power profiles under small $n$ with heavy-tailed distributions: the percentile bootstrap CI is based on the resampled median distribution and is less sensitive to tail observations, while the Wilcoxon signed-rank test uses signed ranks and is more sensitive to tail outliers. This inconsistency itself supports the conservative ``mixed/weak evidence'' interpretation.}\n\nThe absolute magnitude is architecture-dependent: LLaMA severity differences are ${\\sim}40{\\times}$ smaller than DeepSeek and ${\\sim}7{\\times}$ smaller than Qwen. Notably, random eviction on LLaMA also exhibits a non-trivial phase gradient ($d_z$: $0.35 \\to 0.44$), substantially reducing the strategy contrast that is the hallmark of the phase-strategy interaction on Qwen2 models. This suggests that the strength of the strategy$\\times$phase interaction---not merely the severity gradient itself---is architecture-dependent. The directional pattern---importance producing larger phase gradients than random---is qualitatively consistent across all three models, but the LLaMA evidence should be interpreted as preliminary given the weak interaction magnitude and partial statistical significance.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Cross-architecture validation on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B (LlamaForCausalLM). $\\widetilde{\\Delta}$: median paired difference (importance $-$ random) with bootstrap 95\\% CI ($B{=}10{,}000$); $p$: Bonferroni-corrected Wilcoxon signed-rank $p$ ($k{=}4$). $n{=}49$ paired problems.}\n\\label{tab:llama-cross-arch}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}lcc@{}}\n\\toprule\n$s$ & $\\widetilde{\\Delta}$\\; [95\\% CI] & $p_{\\text{Bonf}}$ \\\\\n\\midrule\n0.15 & $0.020$\\; $[0.002,\\; 0.043]$ & $0.219$ \\\\\n0.20 & $0.022$\\; $[-0.090,\\; 0.187]$ & $0.253$ \\\\\n0.25 & $0.095$\\; $[-0.004,\\; 0.303]$ & $0.083$ \\\\\n0.30 & $0.356$\\; $[-0.069,\\; 0.544]$ & $\\mathbf{0.029}$ \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\paragraph{Post-hoc power analysis.}\nWe computed post-hoc power via bootstrap simulation ($B{=}10{,}000$) to assess what claims the $n{=}49$ sample can and cannot support. For the within-importance phase gradient (testing $\\Delta_{\\text{imp}} > 0$), power exceeds $0.99$ at all four sparsity levels, confirming that the sample is sufficient to detect the importance-based severity gradient. For strategy comparisons (testing $\\Delta_{\\text{imp}} - \\Delta_{\\text{rand}} > 0$), power ranges from $0.26$ ($s{=}0.15$) to $0.63$ ($s{=}0.30$), indicating that the sample is underpowered for detecting strategy-level differences at their observed effect sizes. Extrapolating from the observed effect sizes and variance, $n \\geq 200$ paired problems would be needed to achieve $0.80$ power for the strategy comparison at $s{=}0.30$. 98	\section{Robustness to Asymmetric Sparsity Allocation}
```
```text
99	\label{app:asymmetric-robustness}
100	
101	Our main experiments use symmetric sparsity ($s_{\text{head}} = s_{\text{KV}} = s$). To verify that the phase-gated severity gradient does not depend on this symmetric assumption, we evaluate two asymmetric configurations at a fixed total sparsity budget equivalent to $s{=}0.25$, using importance-based eviction on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B.
102	
103	\begin{table}[h]
104	\centering
105	\small
106	\caption{Asymmetric sparsity robustness: per-phase II under two head/KV allocations (DeepSeek, importance-based eviction, $n{=}50$ spot check).}
107	\label{tab:asymmetric-robust}
108	\begin{tabular}{@{}lcccccc@{}}
109	\toprule
110	Config & $s_{\text{head}}$ & $s_{\text{KV}}$ & $\widetilde{\mathrm{II}}_{\textit{low}}$ & $\widetilde{\mathrm{II}}_{\textit{mid}}$ & $\widetilde{\mathrm{II}}_{\textit{high}}$ & $\Delta$ \\
111	\midrule
112	A (KV-heavy) & 0.20 & 0.30 & 19.51 & 6.05 & 0.38 & $+$19.13 \\
113	B (head-heavy) & 0.30 & 0.20 & 18.77 & 3.97 & 0.52 & $+$18.25 \\
114	\addlinespace
115	Symmetric & 0.25 & 0.25 & 3.97 & --- & 0.02 & $+$3.99 \\
116	\bottomrule
117	\end{tabular}
118	\end{table}
119	
120	Both asymmetric configurations produce large positive $\Delta$ ($+19.13$ and $+18.25$), confirming that the phase-gated severity gradient is robust to the head/KV allocation ratio. The KV-heavy configuration (A) exhibits higher mid-phase II ($6.05$ vs.\ $3.97$), suggesting that the KV eviction axis has a stronger impact on intermediate reasoning phases. Both asymmetric $\Delta$ values exceed the symmetric baseline ($3.99$), consistent with one axis operating at $s{=}0.30$---above the symmetric maximum tested---which amplifies overall severity. A systematic investigation of the full asymmetric interaction surface is left for future work.
121	
122	
123	\section{Outlier Robustness Analysis}
124	\label{app:outlier-robustness}
125	
126	The extreme mean/median ratio (up to $97{,}640{:}1$ in individual conditions) reflects heavy-tailed II distributions driven by near-zero denominators in specific problems. We verify robustness via symmetric winsorization at $5\%$ and $10\%$ levels. All median-based statistics---paired median difference $\Delta$, Wilcoxon signed-rank $p$-values, and amplification ratio $G$---are strictly invariant under winsorization, as expected for rank-based estimators. The five core claims---compound exceeds individual, importance exceeds random, monotonic dose-response, $G \gg 1$ for importance, $G \approx 1$ for random---are fully robust. The extreme outlier severity further validates our choice of median-based inference over mean-based alternatives. $G$ values in this robustness analysis follow the ratio-of-medians convention (Table~\ref{tab:profiling-subset}); the primary $G$ definition (Eq.~\ref{eq:gradient}) is similarly invariant as a median of per-problem ratios.
127	
128	
129	\section{\texttt{&lt;think>} Token Phase Validation}
130	\label{app:think-validation}
131	
132	DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B generates explicit \texttt{&lt;think>}...\texttt{&lt;/think>} delimiters that demarcate chain-of-thought reasoning from final answers, providing a model-generated ground truth for reasoning phase boundaries. We evaluate whether our attention-sparsity-based phase segmentation (\S\ref{par:phase-segmentation}) recovers these boundaries on GSM8K ($n{=}48$ problems with valid phase assignments), using adaptive per-sequence quantile segmentation rather than a fixed global threshold.
133	
134	\paragraph{Naming convention.}
135	A note on terminology: DeepSeek's \texttt{&lt;think>} phase (active reasoning) exhibits \emph{low} attention sparsity---attention is broadly distributed across many positions during exploratory computation. Conversely, the answer phase exhibits \emph{high} attention sparsity---attention concentrates on a few positions during structured output. Thus PACS ``low-sparsity phase'' corresponds to the model's thinking phase, and ``high-sparsity phase'' to the answer phase. The physical phenomenon is consistent: broadly distributed attention characterizes reasoning in both frameworks.
136	
137	\paragraph{Token-level agreement.}
138	We binarize each token as ``thinking'' (inside \texttt{&lt;think>}) or ``answer'' (outside) and compare against the sparsity-based phase assignment. The adaptive segmentation achieves substantial agreement: $\kappa{=}0.88$ (per-problem mean $0.85$, median $0.88$), with token-level accuracy of $94.2\%$. Per-problem AUC-ROC is $0.98$ (median $0.99$), indicating near-perfect discriminability between thinking and answer phases based on attention sparsity alone. Of problems, $75\%$ achieve $\kappa \geq 0.80$ and $100\%$ achieve $\kappa \geq 0.60$. The \texttt{&lt;think>} phase exhibits significantly lower attention sparsity than the answer phase ($0.656$ vs.\ $0.763$; Cohen's $d{=}-0.80$), confirming that attention distribution breadth reliably distinguishes reasoning from structured computation.
139	
140	\paragraph{Boundary proximity.}
141	The median distance between the sparsity-based phase boundary and the nearest \texttt{&lt;think>}/\texttt{&lt;/think>} delimiter is 1 token, with $81.2\%$ of boundaries falling within 10 tokens. This tight alignment indicates that the attention sparsity transition closely tracks the model's self-identified shift from exploratory reasoning to structured answer generation.
142	
143	\paragraph{Phase composition and II correlation.}
144	We examine whether the fraction of thinking tokens within each phase correlates with compound severity (II). At the mid-phase level, the thinking-token fraction correlates with II (Spearman $\rho{=}0.49$, $p < 0.001$), consistent with mid-phase segments that contain more reasoning content being more vulnerable to compound sparsity. Low- and high-phase correlations are non-significant, as expected: these phases are compositionally dominated by thinking or answer tokens, respectively, leaving insufficient variance for correlation.
145	
146	\paragraph{MATH-500 indirect validation.}
147	Direct $\kappa$ validation requires explicit \texttt{&lt;think>} delimiters, available only on GSM8K. On MATH-500 ($n{=}500$), we instead assess \emph{temporal ordering consistency}: the fraction of problems where the low-sparsity phase precedes the high-sparsity phase, consistent with a thinking-then-answering generation structure. DeepSeek achieves a temporal ordering score of $0.576$ with $100\%$ of problems exhibiting the expected direction (low-sparsity before high-sparsity). Qwen, which lacks \texttt{&lt;think>} delimiters, shows weaker but directionally consistent temporal ordering ($0.479$). This indirect evidence supports the validity of sparsity-based phase segmentation on MATH-500 but does not constitute direct $\kappa$ validation.
148	
149	\paragraph{Scope.}
150	The direct $\kappa$ validation ($\kappa{=}0.88$, $n{=}48$) is limited to DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B on GSM8K; MATH-500 has only indirect (temporal ordering) validation. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct does not produce explicit reasoning delimiters, precluding direct $\kappa$ validation on either benchmark. Phase segmentation is computed on the dense model's attention patterns and is independent of sparsity level, so the agreement statistics are reported once rather than per-$s$.
151	
152	
153	\section{Amplification Ratio ($G$) Details}
154	\label{app:g-metric}
155	
156	The per-problem amplification ratio $G^{(i)} = \mathrm{II}^{(i)}_{\textit{low}} / \mathrm{II}^{(i)}_{\textit{high}}$ (Eq.~\ref{eq:gradient}) provides a complementary ratio perspective to the primary metric $\Delta$. Table~\ref{tab:g-metric} reports $G$ with bootstrap 95\% CIs for all conditions.
157	
158	\begin{table*}[t]
159	\centering
160	\small
161	\caption{Per-problem amplification ratio $G$ (median of $G^{(i)}$) with bootstrap 95\% CIs ($B{=}10{,}000$). $G \gg 1$ indicates phase-concentrated severity. Importance-based eviction exhibits strong phase modulation ($G{=}7.5$--$79.8{\times}$ for DeepSeek, $2.6$--$36.9{\times}$ for Qwen); random eviction remains near $G \approx 1$.}
162	\label{tab:g-metric}
163	\begin{tabular}{@{}llcc@{}}
164	\toprule
165	$s$ & Strategy & $G$\; [95\% CI] (DeepSeek) & $G$\; [95\% CI] (Qwen) \\
166	\midrule
167	0.15 & Importance & $\mathbf{7.5}$\; $[4.1,\; 11.4]$ & n.d.$^{\dagger}$ \\
168	0.15 & Random     & 0.5\; $[0.4,\; 0.6]$ & 0.7\; $[0.4,\; 0.8]$ \\
169	\addlinespace
170	0.20 & Importance & $\mathbf{17.9}$\; $[12.7,\; 26.3]$ & $\mathbf{2.6}$\; $[0.5,\; 5.1]$ \\
171	0.20 & Random     & 0.8\; $[0.6,\; 0.9]$ & 0.9\; $[0.7,\; 1.0]$ \\
172	\addlinespace
173	0.25 & Importance & $\mathbf{49.1}$\; $[40.1,\; 57.8]$ & $\mathbf{9.5}$\; $[5.2,\; 14.8]$ \\

182	
183	Strong phase modulation is specific to importance-based eviction ($G{=}7.5$--$79.8{\times}$ for DeepSeek, $2.6$--$36.9{\times}$ for Qwen). Random eviction shows weak but statistically significant modulation at $s \geq 0.25$ ($G \approx 1.1$--$1.3$; CIs exclude 1), while at $s \leq 0.20$ the CIs span or touch 1. The monotonic increase in $G$ with sparsity parallels the $\Delta$ dose-response in Table~\ref{tab:main-results}.
```
